## Method and limits

Experimental conditional probabilities use disjoint preceding history and a separately weighted verified new-ballot batch. Finite beta-binomial tails use normalized log recurrence and an explicit numerical error policy; oversized calculations are withheld. Hand-set sample caps are unvalidated modeling choices.

Remaining pools require contemporaneous official category counts or declared low/base/high sensitivity scenarios, compatible dates and jurisdictions, and explicit contest/pair participation assumptions. Unknown pools remain unestimated; final totals and turnout overruns cannot establish zero outstanding.

The optional 0–100 evidence rubric is a hand-set, unvalidated heuristic. Separate fields expose lifecycle, source age, coverage, provenance, rule support, remaining-pool basis and calibration. Snapshot count is not independent validation, and rubric grades are not correctness probabilities.

When the available file is only a county slice of a multicounty or statewide race, the report marks it noncontrolling and does not treat it as the official outcome forecast.

For statewide XLSX inputs, the ballot denominator is the larger of the reported Ballots Cast value and the auditable sum of valid votes, overvotes, and undervotes. Any adjustment is disclosed on that race.

## Geography: county and precinct results

County snapshots: 1; precinct snapshots: 1. Every race's per-county votes are in county-breakdown.csv; precinct rows are in the election's areas/ directory.

Reconciliation of the 2026-09-11T10:37 county snapshot: 507 contest-choice totals compared with the statewide Summary Results, 0 mismatches. Precincts against counties: 5,874 comparisons, 0 mismatches; 151 contest-county pairs have county votes but no precinct rows in the state file.

Data-quality finding: the state precinct file has no precinct rows for 151 contest-county pairs in King County; their precinct-level changes cannot be shown from the state file.

County changes: needs two county snapshots; only 2026-09-11T10:37 is available.

Precinct changes: needs two precinct snapshots; only 2026-09-11T10:37 is available.

No county remaining-ballot counts are configured ([[remaining_ballots]] in election.toml), so no county can be assessed as able to change an outcome.
